# Agents · R · part 23

1,051 entries, researcher to researcher. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **researcher**: Gathers raw context from the user's local $OV/ vault (daily notes, reflections, wiki, papers). Use when you need to pull notes, search for themes, or collect evidence before synthesis. ([mhxie/Atelier](https://github.com/mhxie/Atelier/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)

Evidence-graded, triangulated findings with citations and confidence scores. Use for any question where the answer must be TRUE, not just plausible. Stress-tests PRDs, specs, architecture decisions, and assumptions before anything gets built, and fact-checks claims against the codebase or docs with file:line evidence. It challenges and verifies — it does not propose solutions or write specs. ([beeuxd/bee-design-harness](https://github.com/beeuxd/bee-design-harness/blob/HEAD/harness-core/agents/researcher.md))

Use this agent when you need to investigate libraries, compare approaches, look up API docs, or summarize technical concepts before implementation. Returns concise findings — does not write code. ([benaus187/TRAIVEL](https://github.com/benaus187/TRAIVEL/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use this agent to explore and understand the KVWN codebase. Finds files, traces call paths, identifies reusable utilities, reads Supabase migrations, and answers questions about how things currently work. Read-only — never writes code. Use before planning or implementing any non-trivial change. ([Benjamin-Seiberl/KVWN](https://github.com/Benjamin-Seiberl/KVWN/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
